# FILE: plot_twist/story.py
================================================================================

"""The story of Plot Twist: every room, item, door, object, ending and text of the game.

This file is only data. The rules that read it are in engine.py, and nothing here
changes while the game runs. Ids and texts are in English.
"""

# Where every new game starts.
START_ROOM = "deck"

# The characters the player can choose from when a new game starts, in the order
# the choice shows them. They look the same, except for the colour of the shirt:
# "sprite" is the start of the names of their pictures in sprites.py.
CHARACTERS = {
    "green": {"sprite": "player"},
    "red": {"sprite": "player_red"},
}

# What the game remembers about the player's progress and choices, with the
# value each flag has at the start of a new game.
FLAGS = {
    # The character chosen for this game (one of CHARACTERS): player 1's, in a game for two.
    "character": "green",
    # A game for two players on the same keyboard: player 2 is the other character.
    "two_players": False,
    # The eight generators of the captain's cabin, in a ring: True is on, False is
    # off. The puzzle starts with H, A, C and D off and B, E, F and G on, and goes
    # back to that every time the player walks into the cabin, until the circuit
    # is complete. Pulling A, then C (or C, then A) turns them all on.
    "generator_a": False,
    "generator_b": True,
    "generator_c": False,
    "generator_d": False,
    "generator_e": True,
    "generator_f": True,
    "generator_g": True,
    "generator_h": False,
    # All eight generators were on at once: the barrier in the cabin has dissolved.
    "cabin_solved": False,
    # The numbered tiles of the hold: the phase being played (1, 2 or 3) and how
    # many tiles of its sequence are lit. Then all three phases are done: the
    # barrier around the altar of the second key has dissolved.
    "hold_phase": 1,
    "hold_step": 0,
    "hold_solved": False,
    # The hold puzzle is solved: the player has learned that the crab tells the truth.
    "crab_truthful": False,
    # The final doors, each with its own memory: the door's id once it has been
    # opened, None while it is locked. Opening one does not seal the other.
    "crab_door": None,
    "angel_door": None,
    # The green tincture has been found in the infirmary: the other three have
    # appeared, scattered around the ship (the galley, the helm, the corridor).
    "tinctures_scattered": False,
    # The cauldron of the infirmary: how many ingredients of the recipe are in it,
    # in the right order; then the remedy is ready (and the cauldron empty again).
    "potion_step": 0,
    "potion_solved": False,
    # The doctor drank the remedy: his ghost is gone, and the anvil has appeared in the galley.
    "doctor_at_peace": False,
    # The rooms the player has been in, for the map in the corner of the screen.
    "visited_deck": True,
    "visited_cabin": False,
    "visited_hold": False,
    "visited_crab_room": False,
    "visited_angel_room": False,
    "visited_infirmary": False,
    "visited_galley": False,
    "visited_helm": False,
    "visited_east_room": False,
    "visited_corridor": False,
    "visited_corridor_end": False,
    "visited_mine": False,
}

# Items the player can collect. An item lies in its room, on tile "pos", until it
# is picked up. If "appears_if" names a flag, it shows up only once that flag is true.
# If "free_if" names a flag, it can be seen but not picked up until that flag is true.
# An item with "gone_if" is used up for good once that flag is set.
# An item with "sets_flag" sets that flag when it is picked up (the green tincture
# makes the other three appear).
# An item that is "part_of" another one is fused into it at the anvil: then the
# parts leave the inventory for good, and the whole item takes their place. An
# item that only comes from fusing has no room and no "pos".

# Doors, one per direction: each door is in one room and leads to another, from the
# middle of one wall ("side": N, S, E or W). The way back is on the opposite wall.
# - A door that "requires" items stays locked until one of them is used on it while
#   the player carries them all. Opening it writes the door's id in its
#   "choice_flag". Doors sharing a choice flag would be a choice: opening one would
#   seal the others forever ("sealed_text"). The final doors have a flag each, so
#   both can be opened. Locked doors use "locked_text" and "open_text".
# - A door that "opens_with" an item is barred until the player carries that item,
#   and then it is simply open. "barred_text" says what bars it.

# A door is in the middle of its wall, unless "at" says on which tile its
# opening starts (counted along the wall): a wall can then have more than one door.

# The things that stand in the rooms. Each one stands on tile "pos" (and the
# tiles "size" covers, if it is bigger than one) and blocks the way, except the
# tiles of the floor. "kind" says what it does:
# - "puzzle" and "character" react when the player uses an item on them. A
#   reaction either sets a flag ("set_flag" and "value") or ends the game
#   ("ending"), and always has a "text". "done_text" replaces "look_text" once a
#   puzzle is solved.
# - "switch" is a generator of a circuit (see CIRCUITS): activating it flips the
#   flags in "toggles", its own included, and shows "toggle_text".
# - "tile" is a numbered tile of a floor puzzle (see FLOOR_PUZZLES): the player
#   walks on it, and it lights up when stepped on in the right order.
# - "barrier" stands in the way until its "open_if" flag is true, then it is gone.
# - "altar" holds an item: the cabin's altar.
# - "anvil" holds an item too, and fuses the parts of an item (see "part_of" in
#   ITEMS) when one of them is used on it while the player carries them all.
# - "cauldron" is where a potion puzzle is brewed: the tinctures of its recipe
#   (items) are used on it, one at a time. The remedy lies in it when ready.
# - Any object can have "appears_if" (it stands only once the flag is set, like
#   the anvil) and "gone_if" (it is gone once the flag is set, like the doctor).
# - "portal" is a ring of light on the floor: walking into it takes the player
#   to the portal named in "to", in another room, wherever that is. A portal
#   whose "to" is None only receives: nothing happens near it.
# - "decor" is furniture: E ignores it, unless it is a "clue" (a note to read),
#   which E offers to "Examine". It blocks the way if "solid" is true; if not, the
#   player walks over it (a rope on the floor). "picture" names its sprite, so
#   many pieces can share one.
# The barrel of the hold is still a simple puzzle: it only asks for the wand.
OBJECTS = {
    # The generators of the cabin, in a ring around a square: A top left, B top
    # middle, C top right, then D, E, F, G and H on round the square. Each one is
    # wired to the two next to it in the ring, and to no other.
    "generator_a": {
        "kind": "switch",

        "label": "A",

# Circuits of generators. When every switch of a circuit is on at the same time,
# the circuit is complete: "solved_flag" becomes true and "solved_text" is shown.
# From then on its switches stay on. Until then, they go back to their starting
# value (in FLAGS) every time the player walks into "room".
CIRCUITS = {
    "cabin_circuit": {
        "room": "cabin",
        "switches": ["generator_a", "generator_b", "generator_c", "generator_d",
                     "generator_e", "generator_f", "generator_g", "generator_h"],
        "solved_flag": "cabin_solved",
        "solved_text": "The electric flow is complete! The barrier has dissolved!",
    },
}

# Floor puzzles: numbered tiles to walk on in a given order, one phase after the
# other. Each phase is a sequence of "steps" (tile ids):
# - walking onto the next tile of the sequence lights it up ("lit_text");
# - at the end of a phase, the next one starts from the tile the player stands
#   on, which stays lit ("phase_text"); after the last one, "solved_flag" becomes
#   true and "solved_text" is shown;
# - walking onto any other tile ("wrong_text"), or out of the "zone" around the
#   tiles ("left_text"), switches the lights off: the phase starts over from its
#   first tile. Stepping back onto the tile lit last changes nothing.
# "phase_flag" holds the phase being played, from 1; "step_flag" how many tiles
# of its sequence are lit. The "zone" (column, row, width and height in tiles) is
# for main.py: the engine only hears that the player walked out of it.
FLOOR_PUZZLES = {
    "hold_tiles": {
        "room": "hold",
        "zone": (2, 4, 11, 3),
        "phases": [
            {"name": "The Wave", "steps": ["tile_1", "tile_2", "tile_3", "tile_4", "tile_5"]},
            {"name": "The Return", "steps": ["tile_5", "tile_3", "tile_1"]},
            {"name": "The Short Circuit", "steps": ["tile_1", "tile_5", "tile_2", "tile_4", "tile_3"]},
        ],
        "phase_flag": "hold_phase",
        "step_flag": "hold_step",
        "solved_flag": "hold_solved",
        # Short texts: they show in the banner while the player keeps walking.
        "lit_text": "Click! Tile {label} lit.",
        "phase_text": "Ding! Phase {number}: {name}.",
        "wrong_text": "BZZZ! Wrong tile.",
        "left_text": "BZZZ! You left the tiles.",
        "solved_text": "The hold's system unlocks! The barrier has dissolved!",
    },
}

# The endings. "good" lets the final screen pick its colors.
ENDINGS = {
    "saved": {
        "title": "Everyone saved",
        "text": [
            "The crab taps the wand on the deck, and the sea grows calm.",
            "At dawn, a coast appears on the horizon.",
            "The ship docks: everyone on board is safe.",
        ],
        "good": True,
    },
    "explosion": {
        "title": "The ship explodes",
        "text": [
            "The angel snaps the wand in two.",
            "A blinding light, then a roar.",
            "Of the ship, only smoke on the water remains.",
        ],
        "good": False,
    },
}

# Potion puzzles. The ingredients are items: the player uses them on the
# cauldron one at a time, and the order matters. The next one of the "recipe"
# moves the "step_flag" on; any other spoils the mixture, and it starts over
# from an empty cauldron, the bottles still in hand. With the whole recipe in,
# the "solved_flag" is set: the bottles are used up, the remedy appears in the
# cauldron (an item with "appears_if" on that flag), and the cauldron stays done.
POTION_PUZZLES = {
    "remedy": {
        "room": "infirmary",
        "cauldron": "cauldron",
        "recipe": ["green_tincture", "red_tincture", "gold_tincture", "blue_tincture"],
        # What the player reads when a tincture goes in at the right moment (the last
        # one shows "solved_text" instead).
        "pour_texts": {
            "green_tincture": "You pour the green tincture. The mixture bubbles and turns the colour of seaweed.",
            "red_tincture": "You pour the red tincture. The mixture hisses and turns brown.",
            "gold_tincture": "You pour the gold tincture. The mixture thickens and starts to shine.",
            "blue_tincture": "You pour the blue tincture.",
        },
        "step_flag": "potion_step",
        "solved_flag": "potion_solved",
        "wrong_text": (
            "The {name} hits the mixture with a hiss, and the cauldron spits everything out. "
            "It is empty again: you have to start over."
        ),
        "solved_text": (
            "You pour the blue tincture last. The mixture goes still, turns clear, and glows softly. "
            "The remedy is ready, in the cauldron."
        ),
    },
}

# Clues shown in the inventory once their flag is true.
CLUES = {
    "crab_truthful": "The crab never lies.",
}

# Texts that do not belong to a single room, item, door or object.
MESSAGES = {

================================================================================
# FILE: plot_twist/engine.py
================================================================================

"""The rules of Plot Twist: what the player can do, and what changes when they do it.

A game state is a dict that holds exactly what gets saved:

    room       id of the current room
    inventory  ids of the collected items, in the order they were picked up
    flags      the current value of every flag in story.FLAGS
    ending     id of the ending reached, or None while the game goes on

Everything else (what lies on the floor, which doors are open, which puzzles are
solved) is worked out from those four keys and from story.py. That is why an item
can never be collected twice: once it is in the inventory, it is no longer on the floor.

Every action returns (state, outcome) and never changes the state it receives:
an accepted action returns a changed copy, a refused one returns the same state.
The outcome is a dict:

    ok     True if the action was accepted; main.py saves the game only then
    event  a short code for the tests and the screens, e.g. "picked_up" or "locked"
    text   what to show the player; never empty

The engine knows nothing about pixels, keys or files: main.py decides what is near
the player, and tells the engine when the player steps onto a numbered tile or
walks out of the zone around the tiles; the engine decides whether an action is
allowed and what it changes.
An id that does not exist is a bug in the calling code, not a player's mistake,
so it raises ValueError instead of being refused.
"""

import copy

import story

# The keys of a game state, and of a valid saved game.
STATE_KEYS = {"room", "inventory", "flags", "ending"}


# --- Starting, restarting and resuming ---------------------------------------


def new_game(character=None, two_players=False):
    """Return the state of a brand-new game: on the deck, empty-handed, nothing decided.

    `character` is one of story.CHARACTERS; without it, the first one. With
    `two_players`, it is a game for two on the same keyboard.
    """
    state = {
        "room": story.START_ROOM,
        "inventory": [],
        # A copy: changing this game's flags must never change story.FLAGS.
        "flags": copy.deepcopy(story.FLAGS),
        "ending": None,
    }
    if character is not None:
        if character not in story.CHARACTERS:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown character: {character!r}")
        state["flags"]["character"] = character
    state["flags"]["two_players"] = bool(two_players)
    return state


def start_new_game(existing, confirmed=False, character=None, two_players=False):
    """Start a new game, asking for confirmation first if there is progress to lose.

    `existing` is the current or saved game, or None if there is none. Without
    confirmation it comes back untouched, with the question to ask the player.
    `character` is the character chosen for the new game, and `two_players`
    whether it is a game for two.
    """
    if existing is not None and not confirmed:
        return _refuse(existing, "needs_confirmation", story.MESSAGES["confirm_restart"])
    return _accept(new_game(character, two_players), "started", story.MESSAGES["intro"])


def menu_options(existing):
    """Return the entries of the start menu: "resume" only when there is a game to resume."""
    options = ["new_game"]
    if existing is not None:
        options.append("resume")
    return options


def restore_state(data):
    """Check a game read from a save file and return a clean copy of it, or None if it is not valid.

    Flags missing from the save (because they were added to the story later)
    get their starting value. Anything unknown makes the whole save invalid.
    """
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or set(data) != STATE_KEYS:
        return None
    if not _is_id(data["room"], story.ROOMS):
        return None

    inventory = data["inventory"]
    if not isinstance(inventory, list):
        return None
    for item in inventory:
        if not _is_id(item, story.ITEMS):
            return None
    if len(set(inventory)) != len(inventory):
        return None
    for item in inventory:
        if story.ITEMS[item]["part_of"] in inventory:
            return None  # a part next to the item it was fused into: impossible

    flags = data["flags"]
    if not isinstance(flags, dict):
        return None
    for flag, value in flags.items():
        if flag not in story.FLAGS or not _is_allowed(flag, value):
            return None

    ending = data["ending"]
    if ending is not None and not _is_id(ending, story.ENDINGS):
        return None

    for item in inventory:
        gone_if = story.ITEMS[item].get("gone_if")
        if gone_if is not None and flags.get(gone_if, story.FLAGS[gone_if]):
            return None  # an item used up for good cannot be carried
        appears_if = story.ITEMS[item]["appears_if"]
        if appears_if is not None and not flags.get(appears_if, story.FLAGS[appears_if]):
            return None  # nor one that has not appeared yet
    state = new_game()
    state["room"] = data["room"]
    state["inventory"] = list(inventory)
    state["flags"].update(flags)
    state["ending"] = ending
    if not _floor_puzzles_consistent(state["flags"]):
        return None
    if not _potion_puzzles_consistent(state["flags"]):
        return None
    return state


# --- Looking at the game (nothing changes) -----------------------------------


def is_over(state):
    """True once an ending has been reached."""
    return state["ending"] is not None


def partner_character(state):
    """Player 2's character: the first one of story.CHARACTERS that player 1 did not choose."""
    for character in story.CHARACTERS:
        if character != state["flags"]["character"]:
            return character
    raise ValueError("There is only one character")


def has_item(state, item):
    """True if the item is in the inventory."""
    return item in state["inventory"]


def door_status(state, door):
    """Return "open", "barred", "locked" or "sealed".

    A barred door stays shut until the player carries the item it "opens_with",
    or what that item was fused into; then it is open. A door without a lock is always open. A locked door
    remembers being opened in its choice flag: it is open if the flag holds this
    door, sealed if it holds another door sharing the flag, and locked while the
    flag is None.
    """
    _expect(door, "door")
    info = story.DOORS[door]
    if info["opens_with"] is not None and not _has_or_fused(state, info["opens_with"]):
        return "barred"
    if not info["requires"]:
        return "open"
    chosen = state["flags"][info["choice_flag"]]
    if chosen is None:
        return "locked"
    if chosen == door:
        return "open"
    return "sealed"


def things_here(state):
    """List what is in the current room right now, as dicts with "id", "kind" and "status".

    Items are listed only while they lie on the floor, and a barrier only while
    it stands. The status is "on_floor" for items, or "guarded" if they cannot be
    picked up yet; "solved" or "unsolved" for puzzles; "on" or "off" for switches;
    "lit" or "dark" for tiles; "up" for barriers; "present" for characters and
    altars; and "open", "barred", "locked" or "sealed" for doors.
    """
    room = state["room"]
    things = []
    for item in story.ITEMS:
        if _on_floor(state, item):
            status = "on_floor" if _is_free(state, item) else "guarded"
            things.append({"id": item, "kind": "item", "status": status})
    for thing, info in story.OBJECTS.items():
        if info["room"] == room and _is_standing(state, thing):
            things.append({"id": thing, "kind": info["kind"], "status": _object_status(state, thing)})
    for door, info in story.DOORS.items():
        if info["room"] == room:
            things.append({"id": door, "kind": "door", "status": door_status(state, door)})
    return things


def actions_for(state, target):
    """List the actions the E key offers near a target: anything in the room, or the room itself.

    Each action is a dict with "verb", "target", "item", "label" and
    "irreversible", and perform() accepts it as it is. Using or giving is offered
    for every item in the inventory, not only the right one, so the menu does not
    give the solution away. After an ending nothing is offered.

    E only acts on what matters to the story: the room itself and decor offer
    nothing, so E does nothing there. Decor that is a "clue" (the doctor's notes)
    can still be read.
    """
    kind = _kind(target)
    if kind == "floor" or is_over(state) or not _is_here(state, target):
        return []  # a floor puzzle is not for E: the player's feet act on its tiles

    if kind == "room":
        return []  # far from everything, E does nothing
    if kind == "item":
        if not _is_free(state, target):
            return [_action("examine", target, story.MESSAGES["label_examine"])]
        label = story.MESSAGES["label_pick_up"].format(item=story.ITEMS[target]["name"])
        return [_action("pick_up", target, label)]
    if kind == "switch":
        label = story.MESSAGES["label_activate"].format(name=story.OBJECTS[target]["name"])
        return [_action("activate", target, label)]
    if kind == "portal" and story.OBJECTS[target]["to"] is None:
        return []  # a portal that only receives: nothing happens near it
    if kind == "decor":
        if story.OBJECTS[target].get("clue"):
            return [_action("examine", target, story.MESSAGES["label_examine"])]  # a note to read
        return []  # just furniture: E ignores it
    if kind == "portal":
        return [_action("examine", target, story.MESSAGES["label_examine"])]  # a portal is for the feet
    if kind == "door" and door_status(state, target) == "open":
        destination = story.ROOMS[story.DOORS[target]["to"]]["name"]
        label = story.MESSAGES["label_enter"].format(room=destination)
        return [_action("enter", target, label)]

    # A closed door, a puzzle, a character, a barrier or an altar: look at it, or use an item on it.
    if kind == "character":
        actions = [_action("examine", target, story.MESSAGES["label_talk"])]
        use_label = story.MESSAGES["label_give"]
    else:
        actions = [_action("examine", target, story.MESSAGES["label_examine"])]
        use_label = story.MESSAGES["label_use"]
    for item in state["inventory"]:
        label = use_label.format(item=story.ITEMS[item]["name"])
        if kind == "cauldron" and _potion_puzzle_of(item) is not None:
            label = story.MESSAGES["label_pour"].format(name=story.ITEMS[item]["name"])
        irreversible = _is_irreversible(state, item, target)
        actions.append(_action("use_item", target, label, item, irreversible))
    return actions


def clues(state):
    """Return the texts of the clues found so far, to show in the inventory."""
    found = []
    for flag, text in story.CLUES.items():
        if state["flags"][flag]:
            found.append(text)
    return found


# --- Actions: each one returns (state, outcome) ------------------------------


def pick_up(state, item):
    """Pick up an item lying on the floor of the current room."""
    _expect(item, "item")
    if is_over(state):
        return _refuse(state, "game_over", story.MESSAGES["game_over"])
    if has_item(state, item):
        return _refuse(state, "already_have", story.MESSAGES["already_have"])
    if not _on_floor(state, item):
        return _refuse(state, "not_here", story.MESSAGES["not_here"])
    if not _is_free(state, item):
        return _refuse(state, "guarded", story.MESSAGES["guarded"])
    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    new["inventory"].append(item)
    flag = story.ITEMS[item].get("sets_flag")
    if flag is not None:
        new["flags"][flag] = True  # the green tincture: the other three appear around the ship
    return _accept(new, "picked_up", story.ITEMS[item]["pickup_text"])


def enter(state, door):
    """Go through a door of the current room, if it is open."""
    _expect(door, "door")
    if is_over(state):
        return _refuse(state, "game_over", story.MESSAGES["game_over"])
    info = story.DOORS[door]
    if info["room"] != state["room"]:
        return _refuse(state, "not_here", story.MESSAGES["not_here"])
    status = door_status(state, door)
    if status == "barred":
        return _refuse(state, "barred", info["barred_text"])
    if status == "locked":
        return _refuse(state, "locked", info["locked_text"])
    if status == "sealed":
        return _refuse(state, "sealed", info["sealed_text"])
    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    _leave_floor_puzzles(new)  # walking out of a room is walking out of its tile zones
    new["room"] = info["to"]
    new["flags"]["visited_" + info["to"]] = True  # for the map
    _reset_circuits(new)
    return _accept(new, "entered", story.ROOMS[info["to"]]["name"])


def activate(state, switch):
    """Activate a generator: it and the generators wired to it switch over.

    Activating flips (NOT) every flag in the generator's "toggles": its own and
    those of the two generators next to it. Then the whole circuit is checked:
    if every generator is on at the same time, the circuit is complete, its flag
    is set (so the barrier it feeds dissolves) and the generators stay on from
    then on. The flags are plain booleans: True is on, False is off.
    """
    _expect(switch, "switch")
    if is_over(state):
        return _refuse(state, "game_over", story.MESSAGES["game_over"])
    if not _is_here(state, switch):
        return _refuse(state, "not_here", story.MESSAGES["not_here"])
    circuit = _circuit_of(switch)
    if state["flags"][circuit["solved_flag"]]:
        return _refuse(state, "already_done", story.MESSAGES["circuit_done"])
    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    for flag in story.OBJECTS[switch]["toggles"]:
        new["flags"][flag] = not new["flags"][flag]
    if _circuit_complete(new, circuit):
        new["flags"][circuit["solved_flag"]] = True
        return _accept(new, "circuit_complete", circuit["solved_text"])
    return _accept(new, "toggled", story.OBJECTS[switch]["toggle_text"])


def step_on(state, tile):
    """The player walks onto a numbered tile of a floor puzzle: main.py calls this when it happens.

    The puzzle is a small state machine. Its state is two flags: the phase being
    played (from 1) and the step, that is how many tiles of the phase's sequence
    are already lit. Walking onto:
    - the next tile of the sequence lights it up ("tile_lit");
    - the last tile of the sequence completes the phase ("phase_complete"): the
      next phase starts from this tile, which stays lit. After the last phase the
      puzzle is solved ("tiles_complete"), and the barrier it feeds dissolves;
    - the tile lit last changes nothing ("already_lit"): the player only stepped
      back onto it;
    - any other tile is a mistake ("tiles_reset"): the lights go out and the
      phase starts over from its first tile.
    """
    _expect(tile, "tile")
    if is_over(state):
        return _refuse(state, "game_over", story.MESSAGES["game_over"])
    if not _is_here(state, tile):
        return _refuse(state, "not_here", story.MESSAGES["not_here"])
    puzzle = _floor_puzzle_of(tile)
    if state["flags"][puzzle["solved_flag"]]:
        return _refuse(state, "already_done", story.MESSAGES["tiles_done"])
    phase = state["flags"][puzzle["phase_flag"]]
    step = state["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]]
    sequence = puzzle["phases"][phase - 1]["steps"]
    if step > 0 and tile == sequence[step - 1]:
        return _refuse(state, "already_lit", story.MESSAGES["already_lit"])

    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    if tile != sequence[step]:
        # A wrong tile: the lights go out, and the same phase starts over.
        new["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]] = 0
        return _accept(new, "tiles_reset", puzzle["wrong_text"])
    step += 1
    new["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]] = step
    if step < len(sequence):
        label = story.OBJECTS[tile]["label"]
        return _accept(new, "tile_lit", puzzle["lit_text"].format(label=label))
    if phase == len(puzzle["phases"]):
        # The last tile of the last phase.
        new["flags"][puzzle["solved_flag"]] = True
        return _accept(new, "tiles_complete", puzzle["solved_text"])
    # The phase is complete: the next one starts from this tile, already lit.
    following = puzzle["phases"][phase]  # phases count from 1, the list from 0: this is the next one
    new["flags"][puzzle["phase_flag"]] = phase + 1
    new["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]] = 1 if following["steps"][0] == tile else 0
    text = puzzle["phase_text"].format(number=phase + 1, name=following["name"])
    return _accept(new, "phase_complete", text)


def leave_zone(state, floor_puzzle):
    """The player walks out of the zone around a floor puzzle's tiles: main.py calls this when it happens.

    It is a mistake like a wrong tile: the lights go out and the phase starts
    over ("tiles_reset"). If no tile of the phase is lit, there is nothing to
    start over, and nothing happens.
    """
    _expect(floor_puzzle, "floor")
    if is_over(state):
        return _refuse(state, "game_over", story.MESSAGES["game_over"])
    puzzle = story.FLOOR_PUZZLES[floor_puzzle]
    if puzzle["room"] != state["room"]:
        return _refuse(state, "not_here", story.MESSAGES["not_here"])
    if state["flags"][puzzle["solved_flag"]]:
        return _refuse(state, "already_done", story.MESSAGES["tiles_done"])
    if state["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]] == 0:
        return _refuse(state, "nothing_happens", story.MESSAGES["nothing_happens"])
    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    new["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]] = 0
    return _accept(new, "tiles_reset", puzzle["left_text"])


def use_item(state, item, target):
    """Use an item from the inventory on something in the room: a door, a puzzle, a character,
    the anvil or the cauldron.

    On anything else (a generator, a tile, a barrier, an altar) nothing happens.

    The item stays in the inventory, even after giving it away or opening a door
    with it. Only the anvil (it fuses parts into a whole), the cauldron (the
    finished remedy uses up its tinctures) and a reaction that "consumes" change it.
    """
    _expect(item, "item")
    kind = _kind(target)
    _not_a_floor_puzzle(target)
    if is_over(state):
        return _refuse(state, "game_over", story.MESSAGES["game_over"])
    if not has_item(state, item):
        return _refuse(state, "no_item", story.MESSAGES["no_item"])
    if not _is_here(state, target):
        return _refuse(state, "not_here", story.MESSAGES["not_here"])
    if kind == "door":
        return _use_on_door(state, item, target)
    if kind == "puzzle" or kind == "character":
        return _use_on_object(state, item, target)
    if kind == "anvil":
        return _use_on_anvil(state, item, target)
    if kind == "cauldron":
        return _use_on_cauldron(state, item, target)
    return _refuse(state, "nothing_happens", story.MESSAGES["nothing_happens"])


def examine(state, target):
    """Look at something in the room, or at the room itself, and return what the player sees.

    Nothing changes, but the action is accepted: it shows new information.
    """
    kind = _kind(target)
    _not_a_floor_puzzle(target)
    if is_over(state):
        return _refuse(state, "game_over", story.MESSAGES["game_over"])
    if not _is_here(state, target):
        return _refuse(state, "not_here", story.MESSAGES["not_here"])
    if kind == "room":
        text = story.ROOMS[target]["description"]
    elif kind == "item":
        text = story.ITEMS[target]["description"]
    elif kind == "door":
        text = _door_text(state, target)
    elif kind == "puzzle" and _is_solved(state, target):
        text = story.OBJECTS[target]["done_text"]
    else:
        text = story.OBJECTS[target]["look_text"]
    return _accept(state, "info", text)


def travel(state, portal):
    """The player walks into a portal: main.py calls this when it happens.

    It takes them to the room of the portal it is linked to ("to"), as a door
    would: the floor puzzles they were in start over, and the room goes on the map.
    A portal that only receives ("to" is None, like the one in the helm) goes nowhere.
    """
    _expect(portal, "portal")
    if is_over(state):
        return _refuse(state, "game_over", story.MESSAGES["game_over"])
    if not _is_here(state, portal):
        return _refuse(state, "not_here", story.MESSAGES["not_here"])
    if story.OBJECTS[portal]["to"] is None:
        return _refuse(state, "nothing_happens", story.MESSAGES["nothing_happens"])
    destination = story.OBJECTS[story.OBJECTS[portal]["to"]]["room"]
    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    _leave_floor_puzzles(new)
    new["room"] = destination
    new["flags"]["visited_" + destination] = True
    _reset_circuits(new)
    return _accept(new, "teleported", story.MESSAGES["portal_text"])


def visited_rooms(state):
    """The rooms the player has been in, the current one included: what the map shows."""
    rooms = []
    for room in story.ROOMS:
        if state["flags"]["visited_" + room] or room == state["room"]:
            rooms.append(room)
    return rooms


def is_standing(state, thing):
    """True if an object stands in its room right now: it has appeared and it is not gone."""
    return _is_standing(state, thing)


def perform(state, action):
    """Carry out an action given as data, such as one returned by actions_for().

    The action is a dict with a "verb" ("pick_up", "enter", "use_item",
    "examine", "activate", "step_on", "leave_zone" or "travel"), a "target" and, for
    "use_item", an "item". The target of "leave_zone" is a floor puzzle.
    """
    verb = action["verb"]
    if verb == "pick_up":
        return pick_up(state, action["target"])
    if verb == "enter":
        return enter(state, action["target"])
    if verb == "use_item":
        return use_item(state, action["item"], action["target"])
    if verb == "examine":
        return examine(state, action["target"])
    if verb == "activate":
        return activate(state, action["target"])
    if verb == "step_on":
        return step_on(state, action["target"])
    if verb == "leave_zone":
        return leave_zone(state, action["target"])
    if verb == "travel":
        return travel(state, action["target"])
    raise ValueError(f"Unknown verb: {verb!r}")


# --- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------------------


def _accept(state, event, text):
    """An accepted action: return the new state and its outcome."""
    return state, {"ok": True, "event": event, "text": text}


def _refuse(state, event, text):
    """A refused action: return the state untouched and the explanation."""
    return state, {"ok": False, "event": event, "text": text}


def _action(verb, target, label, item=None, irreversible=False):
    """Build an action in the shape perform() expects, with what the menu needs to show it."""
    return {"verb": verb, "target": target, "item": item, "label": label, "irreversible": irreversible}


def _kind(thing):
    """Return what an id names: "room", "item", "door", "floor" (a floor puzzle) or the kind of an object."""
    if thing in story.ROOMS:
        return "room"
    if thing in story.ITEMS:
        return "item"
    if thing in story.DOORS:
        return "door"
    if thing in story.FLOOR_PUZZLES:
        return "floor"
    if thing in story.OBJECTS:
        return story.OBJECTS[thing]["kind"]
    raise ValueError(f"Unknown id: {thing!r}")


def _expect(thing, kind):
    """Raise ValueError unless the id names a thing of this kind."""
    if _kind(thing) != kind:
        raise ValueError(f"{thing!r} is not a {kind}")


def _not_a_floor_puzzle(target):
    """Raise ValueError if the target is a floor puzzle: one walks on its tiles, one does not look at it or use things on it."""
    if _kind(target) == "floor":
        raise ValueError(f"{target!r} is a floor puzzle: the player walks on its tiles")


def _is_id(value, table):
    """True if the value is one of the ids of a story table (used to check saved data)."""
    return isinstance(value, str) and value in table


def _is_here(state, thing):
    """True if the player can see the thing in the current room right now (the room itself included)."""
    kind = _kind(thing)
    if kind == "room":
        return thing == state["room"]
    if kind == "item":
        return _on_floor(state, thing)
    if kind == "door":
        return story.DOORS[thing]["room"] == state["room"]
    if kind == "floor":
        return story.FLOOR_PUZZLES[thing]["room"] == state["room"]
    return story.OBJECTS[thing]["room"] == state["room"] and _is_standing(state, thing)


def _has_or_fused(state, item):
    """True if the player carries the item, or the whole it was fused into."""
    whole = story.ITEMS[item]["part_of"]
    return has_item(state, item) or (whole is not None and has_item(state, whole))


def _on_floor(state, item):
    """True if the item lies in the current room: it belongs here, it has appeared, nobody took it.

    A part fused into a whole is gone for good, even though it is no longer in the inventory.
    """
    info = story.ITEMS[item]
    if info["room"] != state["room"]:
        return False
    if info["appears_if"] is not None and not state["flags"][info["appears_if"]]:
        return False
    if info.get("gone_if") is not None and state["flags"][info["gone_if"]]:
        return False  # used up for good, like the remedy once drunk
    return not _has_or_fused(state, item)


def _reaction_done(state, reaction):
    """True if a reaction that sets a flag has already set it."""
    return state["flags"][reaction["set_flag"]] == reaction["value"]


def _is_solved(state, puzzle):
    """True once every reaction of a puzzle has done its job."""
    for reaction in story.OBJECTS[puzzle]["reactions"].values():
        if not _reaction_done(state, reaction):
            return False
    return True


def _is_free(state, item):
    """True if nothing keeps the player from picking up the item (its "free_if" flag, if any, is true)."""
    flag = story.ITEMS[item]["free_if"]
    return flag is None or bool(state["flags"][flag])


def _is_standing(state, thing):
    """True if an object is in its room now.

    A barrier is gone once open. Any object can also wait for a flag before it
    appears ("appears_if", the anvil) or go away once a flag is set ("gone_if", the doctor).
    """
    info = story.OBJECTS[thing]
    if info["kind"] == "barrier":
        return not state["flags"][info["open_if"]]
    if info.get("appears_if") is not None and not state["flags"][info["appears_if"]]:
        return False
    if info.get("gone_if") is not None and state["flags"][info["gone_if"]]:
        return False
    return True


def _object_status(state, thing):
    """The status of an object, as things_here() lists it."""
    info = story.OBJECTS[thing]
    if info["kind"] == "puzzle":
        return "solved" if _is_solved(state, thing) else "unsolved"
    if info["kind"] == "switch":
        return "on" if state["flags"][info["flag"]] else "off"
    if info["kind"] == "tile":
        return "lit" if _tile_is_lit(state, thing) else "dark"
    if info["kind"] == "barrier":
        return "up"
    return "present"


def _circuit_of(switch):
    """The circuit a switch belongs to."""
    for circuit in story.CIRCUITS.values():
        if switch in circuit["switches"]:
            return circuit
    raise ValueError(f"{switch!r} is not in any circuit")


def _circuit_complete(state, circuit):
    """True when every switch of the circuit is on at the same time."""
    for switch in circuit["switches"]:
        if not state["flags"][story.OBJECTS[switch]["flag"]]:
            return False
    return True


def _reset_circuits(state):
    """Put back the starting configuration of the circuits in the current room that are not complete.

    It changes the state it is given, so it is used only on the copy an accepted action is building.
    """
    for circuit in story.CIRCUITS.values():
        if circuit["room"] == state["room"] and not state["flags"][circuit["solved_flag"]]:
            for switch in circuit["switches"]:
                flag = story.OBJECTS[switch]["flag"]
                state["flags"][flag] = story.FLAGS[flag]


def _floor_puzzle_of(tile):
    """The floor puzzle a tile belongs to."""
    for puzzle in story.FLOOR_PUZZLES.values():
        for phase in puzzle["phases"]:
            if tile in phase["steps"]:
                return puzzle
    raise ValueError(f"{tile!r} is not in any floor puzzle")


def _tile_is_lit(state, tile):
    """True if the tile was stepped on in the phase being played, or if its puzzle is solved."""
    puzzle = _floor_puzzle_of(tile)
    flags = state["flags"]
    if flags[puzzle["solved_flag"]]:
        return True
    sequence = puzzle["phases"][flags[puzzle["phase_flag"]] - 1]["steps"]
    return tile in sequence[:flags[puzzle["step_flag"]]]


def _leave_floor_puzzles(state):
    """Switch off the lit tiles of the unsolved floor puzzles in the current room: their phase starts over.

    It changes the state it is given, so it is used only on the copy an accepted action is building.
    """
    for puzzle in story.FLOOR_PUZZLES.values():
        if puzzle["room"] == state["room"] and not state["flags"][puzzle["solved_flag"]]:
            state["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]] = 0


def _floor_puzzles_consistent(flags):
    """True if every unsolved floor puzzle has fewer lit tiles than the sequence of its phase."""
    for puzzle in story.FLOOR_PUZZLES.values():
        if flags[puzzle["solved_flag"]]:
            continue
        sequence = puzzle["phases"][flags[puzzle["phase_flag"]] - 1]["steps"]
        if flags[puzzle["step_flag"]] >= len(sequence):
            return False
    return True


def _potion_puzzles_consistent(flags):
    """True if a solved potion puzzle has an empty cauldron: the remedy has been bottled."""
    for puzzle in story.POTION_PUZZLES.values():
        if flags[puzzle["solved_flag"]] and flags[puzzle["step_flag"]] != 0:
            return False
    return True


def _door_text(state, door):
    """What the player reads when examining a door."""
    info = story.DOORS[door]
    status = door_status(state, door)
    if status == "barred":
        return info["barred_text"]
    if status == "locked":
        return info["locked_text"]
    if status == "sealed":
        return info["sealed_text"]
    destination = story.ROOMS[info["to"]]["name"]
    return story.MESSAGES["door_open"].format(room=destination)


def _use_on_door(state, item, door):
    """Open a locked door with the items it needs. Opening a choice door seals the others.

    The item must be one the lock "requires", and the player must carry all of
    them. A part of a required item (half of the key) does not turn: it has to
    be fused first, and the refusal says so.
    """
    info = story.DOORS[door]
    status = door_status(state, door)
    if status == "open":
        return _refuse(state, "already_open", story.MESSAGES["already_open"])
    if status == "sealed":
        return _refuse(state, "sealed", info["sealed_text"])
    if item not in info["requires"]:
        if story.ITEMS[item]["part_of"] in info["requires"]:
            return _refuse(state, "not_fused", story.MESSAGES["not_fused"])
        return _refuse(state, "nothing_happens", story.MESSAGES["nothing_happens"])
    for needed in info["requires"]:
        if not has_item(state, needed):
            return _refuse(state, "missing_item", story.MESSAGES["missing_item"])
    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    new["flags"][info["choice_flag"]] = door
    return _accept(new, "door_opened", info["open_text"])


def _use_on_anvil(state, item, anvil):
    """Fuse the parts of an item into the whole: one part is used on the anvil, and the player carries them all.

    The parts leave the inventory for good, and the whole takes the place of the first one.
    """
    whole = story.ITEMS[item]["part_of"]
    if whole is None:
        return _refuse(state, "nothing_happens", story.MESSAGES["nothing_happens"])
    parts = [part for part, info in story.ITEMS.items() if info["part_of"] == whole]
    for part in parts:
        if not has_item(state, part):
            return _refuse(state, "missing_item", story.MESSAGES["missing_item"])
    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    first = min(new["inventory"].index(part) for part in parts)
    new["inventory"] = [held for held in new["inventory"] if held not in parts]
    new["inventory"].insert(first, whole)
    return _accept(new, "fused", story.OBJECTS[anvil]["fuse_text"])


def _potion_puzzle_of(item):
    """The potion puzzle whose recipe has this item, or None."""
    for puzzle in story.POTION_PUZZLES.values():
        if item in puzzle["recipe"]:
            return puzzle
    return None


def _use_on_cauldron(state, item, cauldron):
    """Pour a tincture into the cauldron of its potion puzzle.

    Like the tiles, it is a small state machine, with one flag: how many
    ingredients of the recipe are in, in the right order. Pouring:
    - the next one of the recipe adds it ("potion_poured");
    - the last one of the recipe makes the remedy ("potion_ready"): it appears in
      the cauldron, the tinctures are used up, and the puzzle is done;
    - any other one spoils the mixture ("potion_spoiled"): the cauldron is empty
      again, and the recipe starts over. The bottles stay in hand, so the player
      can always try again.
    Anything that is not one of its tinctures does nothing.
    """
    puzzle = _potion_puzzle_of(item)
    if puzzle is None or puzzle["cauldron"] != cauldron:
        return _refuse(state, "nothing_happens", story.MESSAGES["nothing_happens"])
    if state["flags"][puzzle["solved_flag"]]:
        return _refuse(state, "already_done", story.MESSAGES["potion_done"])
    recipe = puzzle["recipe"]
    step = state["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]]
    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    if item != recipe[step]:
        new["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]] = 0
        name = story.ITEMS[item]["name"].lower()
        return _accept(new, "potion_spoiled", puzzle["wrong_text"].format(name=name))
    step += 1
    if step == len(recipe):
        new["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]] = 0  # the cauldron is empty: the remedy is in the bottle
        new["flags"][puzzle["solved_flag"]] = True
        new["inventory"] = [held for held in new["inventory"] if held not in recipe]  # used up
        return _accept(new, "potion_ready", puzzle["solved_text"])
    new["flags"][puzzle["step_flag"]] = step
    return _accept(new, "potion_poured", puzzle["pour_texts"][item])


def _use_on_object(state, item, thing):
    """Apply the reaction a puzzle or a character has to this item, if it has one.

    A reaction with "consumes" takes the item out of the inventory (the doctor drinks the remedy).
    """
    reaction = story.OBJECTS[thing]["reactions"].get(item)
    if reaction is None:
        return _refuse(state, "nothing_happens", story.MESSAGES["nothing_happens"])
    if "ending" in reaction:
        new = copy.deepcopy(state)
        new["ending"] = reaction["ending"]
        return _accept(new, "ending", reaction["text"])
    if _reaction_done(state, reaction):
        return _refuse(state, "already_done", story.MESSAGES["already_done"])
    new = copy.deepcopy(state)
    new["flags"][reaction["set_flag"]] = reaction["value"]
    if reaction.get("consumes"):
        new["inventory"].remove(item)  # drunk, eaten, used up: it is gone
    return _accept(new, "flag_set", reaction["text"])


def _is_irreversible(state, item, target):
    """True if using the item on the target would open a choice door or end the game.

    Actions never change the state they receive, so we can simply try it.
    """
    _, outcome = use_item(state, item, target)
    if outcome["event"] == "ending":
        return True
    return outcome["event"] == "door_opened" and _is_choice_door(target)


def _is_choice_door(door):
    """True if opening this door seals another one, because the two share a choice flag."""
    flag = story.DOORS[door]["choice_flag"]
    if flag is None:
        return False
    sharing = 0
    for info in story.DOORS.values():
        if info["choice_flag"] == flag:
            sharing += 1
    return sharing > 1


def _allowed_values(flag):
    """Every value a flag can take in valid play: its starting value, and what doors, reactions,
    switches, circuits, floor and potion puzzles and the map write."""
    values = [story.FLAGS[flag]]
    for door, info in story.DOORS.items():
        if info["choice_flag"] == flag:
            values.append(door)
    for info in story.OBJECTS.values():
        for reaction in info["reactions"].values():
            if reaction.get("set_flag") == flag:
                values.append(reaction["value"])
        if flag in info.get("toggles", []):
            values += [True, False]  # a switch flips it both ways
    for circuit in story.CIRCUITS.values():
        if circuit["solved_flag"] == flag:
            values.append(True)
    for puzzle in story.FLOOR_PUZZLES.values():
        if puzzle["phase_flag"] == flag:
            values += list(range(1, len(puzzle["phases"]) + 1))
        if puzzle["step_flag"] == flag:
            longest = max(len(phase["steps"]) for phase in puzzle["phases"])
            values += list(range(longest + 1))
        if puzzle["solved_flag"] == flag:
            values.append(True)
    for puzzle in story.POTION_PUZZLES.values():
        if puzzle["step_flag"] == flag:
            values += list(range(len(puzzle["recipe"])))  # never the whole recipe: then it is solved
        if puzzle["solved_flag"] == flag:
            values.append(True)
    for room in story.ROOMS:
        if flag == "visited_" + room:
            values.append(True)
    for info in story.ITEMS.values():
        if info.get("sets_flag") == flag:
            values.append(True)
    if flag == "character":
        values += list(story.CHARACTERS)
    if flag == "two_players":
        values.append(True)
    return values


def _is_allowed(flag, value):
    """True if the flag can hold this value. The types must match too, because in Python 1 == True."""
    for allowed in _allowed_values(flag):
        if value == allowed and type(value) is type(allowed):
            return True
    return False


================================================================================
# FILE: plot_twist/save.py
================================================================================

"""Saving and loading Plot Twist: one small JSON file next to the game.

This module only writes and reads. It does not know the rules: whatever comes
back from the file goes through engine.restore_state() before it is played.
"""

import json
import os

# Change this number when the shape of the save file changes: files written
# with another number are then ignored instead of being misread.
VERSION = 1

# Where the game is saved when no other path is given. The tests point it to
# a temporary folder (see conftest.py), so they never touch the real save.
SAVE_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "savegame.json")


def save_game(state, path=None):
    """Write the game to the save file, replacing the previous save.

    The game goes into a temporary file first, which then takes the place of
    the old one in a single step: if writing breaks halfway, the previous save
    is still there, intact. Errors while writing are not hidden: they are raised.
    """
    # None means SAVE_PATH as it is now. A default of path=SAVE_PATH would be
    # fixed once, when this file is loaded, and the tests could not move it.
    if path is None:
        path = SAVE_PATH
    temporary = str(path) + ".tmp"
    with open(temporary, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
        json.dump({"version": VERSION, "state": state}, file, indent=2)
    os.replace(temporary, path)


def load_game(path=None):
    """Return the game stored in the save file, or None if there is no usable save.

    A missing file, a broken one and one written with another VERSION all give
    None: the menu then simply does not offer to resume.
    """
    if path is None:
        path = SAVE_PATH
    try:
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as file:
            data = json.load(file)
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        # OSError: no file, or it cannot be read. ValueError: it is not valid JSON text.
        return None
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or data.get("version") != VERSION or "state" not in data:
        return None
    return data["state"]


================================================================================
# FILE: plot_twist/main.py
================================================================================

"""Plot Twist on screen: the window, the menus, the rooms and the player.

Run it with:  uv run python main.py

Everything is drawn on a small 256 x 224 screen, then blown up three times
without smoothing, which gives the 8-bit look. The pictures come from sprites.py
and the letters from pixel_font.py; a thing without a picture is drawn as a
colored rectangle.

The rules are not here: main.py asks engine.py what happens, and saves through
save.py after every accepted action. Most actions start from the E key; the
floor puzzles start from the player's feet, with the movement triggers checked
after each step (see floor_triggers). The sound effects come from sounds.py.
The interface keeps its own small state in a dict called `app`: which scene is
showing, where the player stands, what the text box says. The game itself is
app["state"], the engine's state.
"""

import math

import pygame

import effects
import engine
import save
import sounds
import sprites
import story
from pixel_font import PixelFont

# --- Sizes -----------------------------------------------------------------------

WIDTH, HEIGHT = 256, 224   # the small screen everything is drawn on
SCALE = 3                  # the window is three times bigger
FPS = 60
TILE = 16
HUD_HEIGHT = 2 * TILE      # the bar at the top, above the room
COLUMNS, ROWS = 16, 12     # the screen in tiles below the bar, and the size of a room unless story.py says otherwise
VIEW = pygame.Rect(0, HUD_HEIGHT, COLUMNS * TILE, ROWS * TILE)  # where the room is shown on screen

PLAYER_SIZE = 12
PLAYER_SPEED = 1.5         # pixels per frame
SPAWN_GAP = 4              # how far from a door the player appears after walking through it
REACH = 8                  # how close the player must be to a thing to act on it with E
BANNER_FRAMES = 2 * FPS    # how long the room name stays up after entering a room
DIALOG_LINES = 4           # lines of text per page of the text box
DIALOG_PADDING = 8
DIALOG_TEXT_WIDTH = WIDTH - 4 * DIALOG_PADDING  # margin and padding on both sides of the box
FADE_FRAMES = FPS // 4     # how long a room takes to come out of the dark after a door
WALK_STEP_FRAMES = 8       # frames per step of the walking animation
WAVE_FRAMES = FPS // 6     # frames between two pictures of the stormy sea (four of them)
SEA_PICTURES = 4           # pictures of the stormy sea, shown one after the other
CALM_FRAMES = FPS // 2     # frames between the two pictures of the calm sea of the good ending
BARRIER_FRAMES = 6         # frames between the two pictures of a barrier's energy
FLASH_FRAMES = FPS         # how long the tiles blink red after a mistake, before going dark
FLASH_BLINK = 6            # frames between two blinks
ALTAR_LIFT = 5             # how much higher an item lying on an altar is drawn
ENDING_SCENE_HEIGHT = 64   # the picture at the top of the ending screen
ENDING_HORIZON = 40        # where the sea starts in that picture
ENDING_MENU_TOP = 186
EXPLOSION_DELAY = 12       # frames before the ship blows up in the bad ending
EXPLOSION_WHITE = 20       # frames of the white flash of the blast
EXPLOSION_RED = 40         # frames of the red glare after it

# The direction to walk in to go through a door on each wall.
TOWARD = {"N": (0, -1), "S": (0, 1), "W": (-1, 0), "E": (1, 0)}

# --- Keys --------------------------------------------------------------------------

UP_KEYS = (pygame.K_UP, pygame.K_w)
DOWN_KEYS = (pygame.K_DOWN, pygame.K_s)
LEFT_KEYS = (pygame.K_LEFT, pygame.K_a)
RIGHT_KEYS = (pygame.K_RIGHT, pygame.K_d)
CONFIRM_KEYS = (pygame.K_RETURN, pygame.K_KP_ENTER, pygame.K_SPACE, pygame.K_e)

# The keys of each player: to move, to act (E) and to open the inventory.
# Alone, the arrows and WASD both move the one player, as always.
KEYS_1P = [
    {"up": UP_KEYS, "down": DOWN_KEYS, "left": LEFT_KEYS, "right": RIGHT_KEYS,
     "act": (pygame.K_e,), "inventory": (pygame.K_i,)},
]
# Two players on one keyboard: player 1 on the left of it, player 2 on the right.
KEYS_2P = [
    {"up": (pygame.K_w,), "down": (pygame.K_s,), "left": (pygame.K_a,), "right": (pygame.K_d,),
     "act": (pygame.K_e,), "inventory": (pygame.K_q,)},
    {"up": (pygame.K_UP,), "down": (pygame.K_DOWN,), "left": (pygame.K_LEFT,), "right": (pygame.K_RIGHT,),
     "act": (pygame.K_RSHIFT,), "inventory": (pygame.K_p,)},
]

# --- Colors ------------------------------------------------------------------------

COLORS = {
    "black": (12, 12, 20),
    "text": (240, 236, 220),
    "dim_text": (150, 150, 165),
    "highlight": (255, 205, 80),
    "hud": (24, 22, 34),
    "box": (16, 16, 30),
    "sea": (20, 36, 72),
    "foam": (120, 170, 220),
    "railing": (95, 60, 30),
    "player": (90, 200, 120),
    "door_locked": (130, 85, 35),
    "keyhole": (250, 215, 90),
    "door_sealed": (115, 30, 35),
    "seal": (110, 230, 255),
    "good_ending": (24, 50, 90),
    "bad_ending": (80, 20, 20),
    "dawn": (250, 180, 120),
    "dawn_light": (252, 214, 160),
    "land": (52, 128, 80),
    "night": (40, 24, 40),
    "cable": (60, 60, 72),
    "error": (214, 64, 52),
}

# The fallback colors of the numbered tiles, if they have no picture.
TILE_COLORS = {"lit": COLORS["highlight"], "dark": COLORS["cable"], "error": COLORS["error"]}

# The kinds of object an item can lie on: drawn low, before the item, which sits a little higher.
HOLDERS = ("altar", "anvil", "cauldron")

# The sound each outcome of a floor puzzle plays (see sounds.py).
EVENT_SOUNDS = {
    "tile_lit": "blip",
    "phase_complete": "ding",
    "tiles_complete": "fanfare",
    "tiles_reset": "buzz",
    "potion_poured": "blip",
    "potion_spoiled": "buzz",
    "potion_ready": "fanfare",
    "teleported": "ding",
}

# --- The map in the corner ------------------------------------------------------------

INVENTORY_ROOM = 108  # pixels of the bar for the items carried, left of the map
MAP_SCALE = 3        # one pixel of the map for every three tiles of the ship
MAP_HALO = (70, 70, 100)  # the soft border around a room already visited

# Floor and wall color of each room.
ROOM_COLORS = {
    "deck": ((165, 115, 65), COLORS["sea"]),
    "cabin": ((120, 80, 50), (70, 45, 30)),
    "hold": ((70, 60, 55), (40, 32, 30)),
    "crab_room": ((60, 110, 110), (30, 60, 65)),
    "angel_room": ((225, 220, 200), (170, 165, 150)),
    "infirmary": ((200, 210, 205), (110, 125, 120)),
    "galley": ((120, 80, 50), (40, 32, 30)),
    "helm": ((120, 80, 50), (70, 45, 30)),
    "east_room": ((120, 80, 50), (70, 45, 30)),
    "corridor": ((70, 60, 55), (40, 32, 30)),
    "corridor_end": ((70, 60, 55), (40, 32, 30)),
    "mine": ((90, 70, 55), (50, 40, 35)),
}
DEFAULT_ROOM_COLORS = ((100, 100, 100), (50, 50, 50))

# The color of each item and of each thing standing in a room, for when it has no
# sprite. Generators need none: they are bright when on and dim when off. An id
# missing here is drawn in magenta, so something new in story.py shows up even
# before it has a color.
THING_COLORS = {
    "wand": (180, 110, 255),
    "key": (250, 210, 70),
    "second_key": (250, 210, 70),
    "barrier": (110, 230, 255),
    "hold_barrier": (110, 230, 255),
    "altar": (176, 176, 190),
    "anvil": (60, 60, 72),
    "fused_key": (250, 210, 70),
    "hold_puzzle": (110, 75, 45),
    "crab": (230, 90, 55),
    "angel": (250, 250, 235),
}
MISSING_COLOR = (255, 0, 255)


# --- Room geometry: where things are and how the player moves ------------------------


def tile_rect(col, row, width=1, height=1):
    """The rectangle on screen, in pixels, covering some tiles of the room grid."""
    return pygame.Rect(col * TILE, HUD_HEIGHT + row * TILE, width * TILE, height * TILE)


def doors_in(room):
    """The ids of the doors of a room."""
    doors = []
    for door, info in story.DOORS.items():
        if info["room"] == room:
            doors.append(door)
    return doors


def room_size(room):
    """A room's size in tiles, walls included: one screen, unless story.py gives it a "size"."""
    return story.ROOMS[room].get("size", (COLUMNS, ROWS))


def door_tiles(door):
    """The opening of a door in tiles: (column, row, width, height) in its room.

    Two tiles wide, in the middle of its wall, or from tile "at" along the wall.
    """
    info = story.DOORS[door]
    columns, rows = room_size(info["room"])
    side = info["side"]
    if side == "N" or side == "S":
        start = info.get("at", (columns - 2) // 2)
        return (start, 0 if side == "N" else rows - 1, 2, 1)
    start = info.get("at", (rows - 2) // 2)
    return (0 if side == "W" else columns - 1, start, 1, 2)


def door_rect(door):
    """The opening of a door, in pixels."""
    return tile_rect(*door_tiles(door))


def thing_rect(thing):
    """The tiles an item or an object covers: one, or "size" tiles from "pos" (like the barrier)."""
    if thing in story.ITEMS:
        col, row = story.ITEMS[thing]["pos"]
        return tile_rect(col, row)
    info = story.OBJECTS[thing]
    col, row = info["pos"]
    width, height = info.get("size", (1, 1))
    return tile_rect(col, row, width, height)


def wall_rects(room):
    """The solid parts of the walls of a room: the four sides, with a gap where each door is.

    A wall is cut into pieces at its doors, from one end to the other.
    """
    columns, rows = room_size(room)
    sides = {
        "N": tile_rect(0, 0, columns, 1),
        "S": tile_rect(0, rows - 1, columns, 1),
        "W": tile_rect(0, 0, 1, rows),
        "E": tile_rect(columns - 1, 0, 1, rows),
    }
    walls = []
    for side, wall in sides.items():
        openings = sorted(door_rect(door) for door in doors_in(room) if story.DOORS[door]["side"] == side)
        across = side == "N" or side == "S"  # the wall runs left to right, else top to bottom
        start = wall.left if across else wall.top
        for opening in openings + [None]:
            end = (wall.right if across else wall.bottom) if opening is None else (opening.left if across else opening.top)
            if end > start:
                if across:
                    walls.append(pygame.Rect(start, wall.top, end - start, wall.height))
                else:
                    walls.append(pygame.Rect(wall.left, start, wall.width, end - start))
            if opening is not None:
                start = opening.right if across else opening.bottom
    return walls


def solid_rects(state):
    """Everything the player cannot walk through right now: walls, closed doors and whatever stands in the room.

    That is puzzles, characters, generators, altars, and a barrier until it dissolves.

    Items on the floor, the numbered tiles and flat decor are not solid: the player walks over them.
    """
    solids = wall_rects(state["room"])
    for thing in engine.things_here(state):
        if thing["kind"] == "door":
            if thing["status"] != "open":
                solids.append(door_rect(thing["id"]))
        elif thing["kind"] not in ("item", "tile") and not is_flat(thing["id"]):
            solids.append(thing_rect(thing["id"]))
    return solids


def is_flat(thing):
    """True for what lies on the floor and is walked over: a portal, or flat decor like a coil of rope."""
    info = story.OBJECTS.get(thing)
    if info is None:
        return False
    return info["kind"] == "portal" or (info["kind"] == "decor" and not info["solid"])


def player_rect(position):
    """The player's rectangle, from the position of its top-left corner."""
    return pygame.Rect(round(position[0]), round(position[1]), PLAYER_SIZE, PLAYER_SIZE)


def move(position, dx, dy, solids):
    """Move the player by (dx, dy) pixels and return the new position, stopping against solids.

    It moves along x first and then along y, so the player slides along a wall
    instead of sticking to it when walking diagonally.
    """
    x, y = position
    if dx != 0:
        x += dx
        rect = player_rect((x, y))
        for solid in solids:
            if rect.colliderect(solid):
                if dx > 0:
                    rect.right = solid.left
                else:
                    rect.left = solid.right
                x = rect.x
    if dy != 0:
        y += dy
        rect = player_rect((x, y))
        for solid in solids:
            if rect.colliderect(solid):
                if dy > 0:
                    rect.bottom = solid.top
                else:
                    rect.top = solid.bottom
                y = rect.y
    return (x, y)


def sign(number):
    """-1, 0 or 1, like the direction of a movement."""
    if number > 0:
        return 1
    if number < 0:
        return -1
    return 0


def door_touched(room, position, dx, dy):
    """The door the player is walking into, or None.

    That is a door whose opening the player stands in, or one they push against:
    one pixel further in the direction they are going would overlap it. Walking
    along a wall past a door does not count.
    """
    rect = player_rect(position)
    probe = rect.move(sign(dx), sign(dy))
    for door in doors_in(room):
        opening = door_rect(door)
        if rect.colliderect(opening) or probe.colliderect(opening):
            return door
    return None


def spawn_in_front_of(door):
    """Where the player appears after walking through a door: just in front of the door back, not touching it."""
    opening = door_rect(door)
    rect = pygame.Rect(0, 0, PLAYER_SIZE, PLAYER_SIZE)
    side = story.DOORS[door]["side"]
    if side == "N":
        rect.midtop = (opening.centerx, opening.bottom + SPAWN_GAP)
    elif side == "S":
        rect.midbottom = (opening.centerx, opening.top - SPAWN_GAP)
    elif side == "W":
        rect.midleft = (opening.right + SPAWN_GAP, opening.centery)
    else:
        rect.midright = (opening.left - SPAWN_GAP, opening.centery)
    return (rect.x, rect.y)


def spawn_by_portal(portal):
    """Where the player appears after a portal: just below the portal they arrive at, not touching it."""
    ring = thing_rect(portal)
    rect = pygame.Rect(0, 0, PLAYER_SIZE, PLAYER_SIZE)
    rect.midtop = (ring.centerx, ring.bottom + SPAWN_GAP)
    return (rect.x, rect.y)


def portal_touched(state, position):
    """The portal the player has stepped into, or None: their middle is inside its ring."""
    center = player_rect(position).center
    for thing in engine.things_here(state):
        if thing["kind"] == "portal" and thing_rect(thing["id"]).inflate(-8, -8).collidepoint(center):
            return thing["id"]
    return None


def camera(room, position):
    """The top-left corner, in room pixels, of the part of the room on screen.

    A room the size of the screen does not move. A bigger one follows the player
    and stops at its edges; a smaller one is centred, with black around it.
    """
    columns, rows = room_size(room)
    center = player_rect(position).center
    corner = []
    for size, view, middle in ((columns * TILE, VIEW.width, center[0]), (rows * TILE, VIEW.height, center[1] - HUD_HEIGHT)):
        if size <= view:
            corner.append((size - view) // 2)
        else:
            corner.append(min(max(middle - view // 2, 0), size - view))
    return (corner[0], corner[1])


def spawn_at_entry(room):
    """Where the player appears when a game starts or resumes in a room: the middle of its entry tile."""
    col, row = story.ROOMS[room]["entry"]
    rect = pygame.Rect(0, 0, PLAYER_SIZE, PLAYER_SIZE)
    rect.center = tile_rect(col, row).center
    return (rect.x, rect.y)


def return_door(room, came_from):
    """The door of `room` that leads back to the room the player came from, or None."""
    for door in doors_in(room):
        if story.DOORS[door]["to"] == came_from:
            return door
    return None


# --- The floor puzzles: numbered tiles, and the zone around them -----------------------


def floor_puzzles_in(room):
    """The ids of the floor puzzles of a room."""
    return [puzzle for puzzle, info in story.FLOOR_PUZZLES.items() if info["room"] == room]


def zone_rect(puzzle):
    """The zone around a floor puzzle's tiles: walking out of it starts the phase over."""
    col, row, width, height = story.FLOOR_PUZZLES[puzzle]["zone"]
    return tile_rect(col, row, width, height)


def tile_under(state, position):
    """The numbered tile the player stands on, the one under the middle of their body, or None."""
    middle = player_rect(position).center
    for thing in engine.things_here(state):
        if thing["kind"] == "tile" and thing_rect(thing["id"]).collidepoint(middle):
            return thing["id"]
    return None


def zones_around(state, position):
    """The floor puzzles of the room whose zone has the middle of the player's body in it."""
    middle = player_rect(position).center
    return {puzzle for puzzle in floor_puzzles_in(state["room"]) if zone_rect(puzzle).collidepoint(middle)}


# --- What the player can act on with E -----------------------------------------------


def reach_rect(thing):
    """Where a thing is, to tell whether the player can reach it: its tile, or the opening of a door."""
    if thing in story.DOORS:
        return door_rect(thing)
    return thing_rect(thing)


def nearest_target(state, position):
    """The thing within reach of the player that is closest to them, or None.

    Only things E can act on count: not the numbered tiles (the feet act on them),
    nor plain decor or a portal that only receives. So a barrel next to the
    generator never gets in the way of the lever.
    """
    body = player_rect(position)
    reach = body.inflate(2 * REACH, 2 * REACH)
    nearest = None
    nearest_distance = 0
    for thing in engine.things_here(state):
        if thing["kind"] == "tile" or not engine.actions_for(state, thing["id"]):
            continue
        rect = reach_rect(thing["id"])
        if not reach.colliderect(rect):
            continue
        distance = (rect.centerx - body.centerx) ** 2 + (rect.centery - body.centery) ** 2
        if nearest is None or distance < nearest_distance:
            nearest = thing["id"]
            nearest_distance = distance
    return nearest


def target_and_actions(state, position):
    """What E would act on, and the actions it offers: the nearest thing, or else the room (which offers nothing)."""
    target = nearest_target(state, position)
    if target is None:
        target = state["room"]
    return target, engine.actions_for(state, target)


def target_name(target):
    """The name of a thing as the player sees it."""
    if target in story.ITEMS:
        return story.ITEMS[target]["name"]
    if target in story.OBJECTS:
        return story.OBJECTS[target]["name"]
    if target in story.DOORS:
        return story.MESSAGES["door"]
    return story.ROOMS[target]["name"]


def prompt(state, position):
    """The hint at the top of the screen: what E does here. With several actions, the thing's name."""
    target, actions = target_and_actions(state, position)
    if not actions:
        return ""
    if len(actions) == 1:
        return story.MESSAGES["prompt"].format(action=actions[0]["label"])
    return story.MESSAGES["prompt"].format(action=target_name(target))


def inventory_lines(state):
    """What the inventory screen lists, as (style, text) pairs: the items and their descriptions, then the clues."""
    lines = item_lines(state)
    found = engine.clues(state)
    if found:
        lines.append(("heading", story.MESSAGES["clues"]))
        for clue in found:
            lines.append(("text", clue))
    return lines


def item_lines(state):
    """The items carried, as (style, text) pairs: each name, then its description."""
    if not state["inventory"]:
        return [("text", story.MESSAGES["inventory_empty"])]
    lines = []
    for item in state["inventory"]:
        lines.append(("name", story.ITEMS[item]["name"]))
        lines.append(("text", story.ITEMS[item]["description"]))
    return lines


def clue_lines(state):
    """The clues found, as (style, text) pairs, in the order of story.CLUES."""
    found = engine.clues(state)
    if not found:
        return [("dim", story.MESSAGES["clues_none"])]
    return [("text", clue) for clue in found]


# --- Text ----------------------------------------------------------------------------


def wrap(text, width, measure):
    """Split a text into lines no wider than `width` pixels. `measure(line)` gives a line's width.

    A single word longer than the width gets a line of its own.
    """
    lines = []
    line = ""
    for word in text.split():
        candidate = word if line == "" else line + " " + word
        if line == "" or measure(candidate) <= width:
            line = candidate
        else:
            lines.append(line)
            line = word
    lines.append(line)
    return lines


def pages(lines, per_page):
    """Group lines into the pages of the text box."""
    grouped = []
    for start in range(0, len(lines), per_page):
        grouped.append(lines[start:start + per_page])
    return grouped


def text_width(font):
    """A function that measures how wide a line of text is in this font."""
    def measure(line):
        return font.size(line)[0]
    return measure


# --- The interface state and what happens in each scene ------------------------------


def new_player(position=(0, 0)):
    """One player walking around the ship: where they are and what their feet and eyes are doing."""
    return {
        "pos": position,         # top-left corner of the player, in pixels
        "facing": (0, 1),        # the direction the player looks at
        "touching": None,        # the door the player is walking into: enter fires once per contact
        "walk": 0,               # frames the player has spent walking, for the walking animation
        "moving": False,         # whether the player moved in the last frame
        "on_tile": None,         # the numbered tile the player stood on in the last frame
        "zones": set(),          # the floor puzzles whose zone the player was in, in the last frame
    }


def new_app(fonts):
    """The state of the interface, starting on the main menu."""
    app = {
        "running": True,
        "fonts": fonts,
        "scene": "menu",         # "menu", "confirm", "mode", "character", "play", "actions", "inventory", "pause" or "ending"
        "menu_index": 0,         # the highlighted entry of the menu on screen
        "existing": None,        # the saved game found when the main menu opened, or None
        "state": None,           # the game being played (the engine's state), or None in the main menu
        "players": [new_player()],  # who walks around the ship: for now one player (see new_player)
        "dialog": [],            # pages of text waiting to be read; the player cannot move meanwhile
        "banner": "",            # room name shown for a moment after entering a room
        "banner_frames": 0,
        "actions": [],           # what the E menu offers (the "actions" scene)
        "actions_title": "",     # the name of the thing the E menu is about
        "question": "",          # what the confirm scene asks
        "confirming": "new_game",  # what "Yes" does: start a new game, or the action waiting for it
        "confirm_back": "menu",  # the scene to go back to if the answer is no
        "character_back": "menu",  # the scene Esc goes back to from the choice of the character
        "mode_back": "menu",     # the scene Esc goes back to from the choice of the mode
        "two_players": False,    # the mode chosen for the new game, until it starts
        "frame": 0,              # frames since the game started, for everything that moves on screen
        "scene_frames": 0,       # frames since the current scene started
        "fade": 0,               # frames left before the room is out of the dark
        "flash_frames": 0,       # frames left of the tiles blinking red after a mistake
        "fx": effects.new_fx(),  # the visual effects: particles, dissolves, the warp (see effects.py)
        "inventory_scroll": 0,   # how many rows the list of items is scrolled down
    }
    open_main_menu(app)
    return app


def set_scene(app, scene, menu_index=0):
    app["scene"] = scene
    app["menu_index"] = menu_index
    app["scene_frames"] = 0


def open_main_menu(app):
    """Show the main menu. "Riprendi" is offered only if the save file holds a valid game."""
    app["state"] = None
    app["existing"] = engine.restore_state(save.load_game())
    app["dialog"] = []
    app["banner_frames"] = 0
    set_scene(app, "menu")


def menu_entries(app):
    """The entries of the menu showing in the current scene: ids, or the actions of the E menu."""
    scene = app["scene"]
    if scene == "menu":
        return engine.menu_options(app["existing"]) + ["quit"]
    if scene == "pause":
        return ["continue", "restart", "main_menu", "quit"]
    if scene == "confirm":
        return ["yes", "no"]
    if scene == "ending":
        return ["new_game", "main_menu"]
    if scene == "mode":
        return ["one_player", "two_players"]
    if scene == "actions":
        return app["actions"]
    return []


def menu_labels(app):
    """What the entries of the current menu say on screen."""
    if app["scene"] == "actions":
        return [action["label"] for action in app["actions"]]
    return [story.MESSAGES["menu_" + entry] for entry in menu_entries(app)]


def choose(app, entry):
    """Do what a menu entry says."""
    if app["scene"] == "actions":
        choose_action(app, entry)
    elif entry == "new_game" or entry == "restart":
        request_new_game(app)
    elif entry == "resume":
        resume_game(app)
    elif entry == "continue":
        set_scene(app, "play")
    elif entry == "main_menu":
        open_main_menu(app)
    elif entry == "quit":
        app["running"] = False
    elif entry == "yes":
        if app["confirming"] == "new_game":
            confirm_new_game(app)
        else:
            set_scene(app, "play")
            do_action(app, app["confirming"])
    elif entry == "no":
        set_scene(app, app["confirm_back"])
    elif entry == "one_player" or entry == "two_players":
        app["two_players"] = entry == "two_players"
        choose_character(app, "mode")  # Esc on the characters comes back to the mode


def go_back(app):
    """What Esc does in a menu."""
    scene = app["scene"]
    if scene == "pause" or scene == "actions":
        set_scene(app, "play")
    elif scene == "confirm":
        set_scene(app, app["confirm_back"])
    elif scene == "ending":
        open_main_menu(app)
    elif scene == "character":
        set_scene(app, app["character_back"])
    elif scene == "mode":
        set_scene(app, app["mode_back"])


def progress(app):
