WARP_FRAMES = 50             # how long the purple warp lasts after a portal

TILE_WAVE_FRAMES = 24        # how long the wave of a lit tile lasts
MISTAKE_SPARKS = 18          # red sparks after a wrong tile

FUSE_FRAMES = 80             # how long the halves of the key take to weld
FUSE_SPARKS = 40             # sparks when they meet
FLASH_FRAMES = 14            # how long a white flash lasts

WAND_RISE_FRAMES = 40        # how long the spark climbs up the arm

CLUE_FRAMES = 75             # how long the glow of a new clue lasts (a bit more than a second)
CLUE_SPARKS = 22             # the twinkles thrown at the start
CLUE_TWINKLE_EVERY = 5       # frames between two twinkles after that
CLUE_GLOW = 16               # radius of the glow

DUST_PER_TILES = 14          # one speck of dust for this many tiles of floor
DUST_MAX = 70

LANTERN_SWING = 7            # degrees a lantern swings either way
LANTERN_GLOW = 14
CRYSTAL_GLOW = 13

# --- Colours ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

SPARK = [(255, 236, 140), (255, 255, 255), (255, 190, 80)]
ENERGY = [(112, 232, 255), (210, 250, 255), (56, 148, 200)]
PURPLE = [(186, 116, 255), (230, 200, 255), (112, 62, 176)]
RED = [(255, 90, 70), (214, 64, 52), (255, 170, 120)]
CLUE = [(255, 236, 140), (255, 255, 255), (150, 230, 255)]  # gold, white and a touch of magic
WELD = [(255, 255, 255), (255, 230, 120), (255, 150, 50), (255, 110, 40)]
LIQUID = {                    # the cauldron, for each step of the recipe
    0: (54, 70, 78),          # nothing yet: dark water
    1: (52, 128, 80),         # the green one: seaweed
    2: (120, 72, 40),         # the red one: brown
    3: (252, 214, 80),        # the gold one: bright gold
    "ready": (190, 245, 255), # the blue one, last: clear and glowing
}
DUST_COLORS = {"mine": (34, 26, 18), "corridor": (22, 26, 30), "hold": (24, 26, 28), "infirmary": (14, 18, 18)}
DUST_DEFAULT = (26, 22, 16)


# --- The state of the effects -----------------------------------------------------------


def new_fx():
    """Everything the effects remember from frame to frame. main.py keeps it in app["fx"]."""
    return {
        "rng": random.Random(SEED),
        "particles": [],     # sparks, bubbles... in room pixels
        "screen": [],        # particles on the screen itself (the warp)
        "dust": [],          # the specks floating in the room
        "room": None,        # the room the dust and the dissolves belong to
        "fading": [],        # barriers dissolving: {"rect", "frames"}
        "waves": [],         # rings spreading from a lit tile: {"center", "age"}
        "fuse": None,        # the halves welding: {"center", "age"}
        "wand_rise": None,   # the spark up the arm: {"age"}
        "clue": None,        # the glow on what gave a new clue: {"rect", "age"}
        "warp": 0,           # frames left of the purple warp
        "flash": 0,          # frames left of the white flash
    }


def reset_room(fx, room, floor, indoor):
    """A new room: forget what belonged to the old one, and scatter the dust of this one."""
    fx["room"] = room
    fx["particles"] = []
    fx["fading"] = []
    fx["waves"] = []
    fx["fuse"] = None
    fx["clue"] = None
    fx["dust"] = []
    if not (FX["dust"] and indoor):
        return
    rng = random.Random(f"{SEED}-{room}")  # the same dust every time the room is entered
    count = min(DUST_MAX, (floor.width // 16) * (floor.height // 16) // DUST_PER_TILES)
    for _ in range(count):
        fx["dust"].append({
            "x": rng.uniform(floor.left, floor.right), "y": rng.uniform(floor.top, floor.bottom),
            "phase": rng.uniform(0, 2 * math.pi), "speed": rng.uniform(0.04, 0.12),
            "bounds": floor, "color": DUST_COLORS.get(room, DUST_DEFAULT),
        })


# --- Particles --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def spawn(fx, x, y, count, colors, speed=1.0, life=30, gravity=0.0, up=0.0, spread=math.pi * 2, angle=0.0,
          layer="particles"):
    """Throw `count` particles from (x, y), in random directions within `spread` around `angle`.

    `up` adds a push upwards, `gravity` pulls them down a little each frame.
    """
    rng = fx["rng"]
    particles = fx[layer]
    for _ in range(count):
        if len(particles) >= MAX_PARTICLES:
            return
        direction = angle + rng.uniform(-spread / 2, spread / 2)
        strength = speed * rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0)
        span = int(life * rng.uniform(0.6, 1.0))
        particles.append({
            "x": float(x), "y": float(y),
            "vx": math.cos(direction) * strength, "vy": math.sin(direction) * strength - up,
            "life": span, "max_life": span, "gravity": gravity, "color": rng.choice(colors),
        })


def update_particles(particles):
    """Move every particle one frame, and drop the ones whose life is over."""
    alive = []
    for p in particles:
        p["x"] += p["vx"]
        p["y"] += p["vy"]
        p["vy"] += p["gravity"]
        p["life"] -= 1
        if p["life"] > 0:
            alive.append(p)
    particles[:] = alive


def draw_particles(surface, particles):
    """Each particle is one pixel, two for the young ones; it darkens as it dies."""
    for p in particles:
        fade = p["life"] / p["max_life"]
        color = tuple(int(c * (0.35 + 0.65 * fade)) for c in p["color"])
        x, y = int(p["x"]), int(p["y"])
        surface.fill(color, (x, y, 1, 1))
        if fade > 0.7:
            surface.fill(color, (x + 1, y, 1, 1))


def update(fx):
    """Move every effect on by one frame."""
    update_particles(fx["particles"])
    update_particles(fx["screen"])
    for speck in fx["dust"]:
        speck["phase"] += 0.02
        speck["x"] += math.sin(speck["phase"]) * speck["speed"]
        speck["y"] -= speck["speed"] * 0.5
        bounds = speck["bounds"]
        if speck["y"] < bounds.top:
            speck["y"] = bounds.bottom - 1  # drifted out of the top: it comes back from below
    for fading in fx["fading"]:
        fading["frames"] -= 1
    fx["fading"] = [fading for fading in fx["fading"] if fading["frames"] > 0]
    for wave in fx["waves"]:
        wave["age"] += 1
    fx["waves"] = [wave for wave in fx["waves"] if wave["age"] < TILE_WAVE_FRAMES]
    if fx["fuse"] is not None:
        fx["fuse"]["age"] += 1
        if fx["fuse"]["age"] >= FUSE_FRAMES:
            fx["fuse"] = None
    if fx["wand_rise"] is not None:
        fx["wand_rise"]["age"] += 1
        if fx["wand_rise"]["age"] >= WAND_RISE_FRAMES:
            fx["wand_rise"] = None
    if fx["clue"] is not None:
        fx["clue"]["age"] += 1
        if fx["clue"]["age"] >= CLUE_FRAMES:
            fx["clue"] = None
    if fx["warp"] > 0:
        fx["warp"] -= 1
    if fx["flash"] > 0:
        fx["flash"] -= 1


def draw_dust(surface, fx, frame):
    """The specks of dust: faint, and twinkling a little."""
    for speck in fx["dust"]:
        shine = 0.6 + 0.4 * math.sin(frame * 0.05 + speck["phase"] * 3)
        color = tuple(int(c * shine) for c in speck["color"])
        surface.fill(color, (int(speck["x"]), int(speck["y"]), 1, 1), special_flags=pygame.BLEND_RGB_ADD)


# --- Light ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

_glows = {}


def glow_picture(radius, color):
    """A round spot of light in four bands, brightest in the middle: drawn once, then kept."""
    key = (radius, color)
    if key not in _glows:
        size = radius * 2 + 1
        picture = pygame.Surface((size, size))
        picture.fill((0, 0, 0))
        for y in range(size):
            for x in range(size):
                distance = math.hypot(x - radius, y - radius) / max(radius, 1)
                if distance < 1:
                    band = math.ceil((1 - distance) * 4) / 4  # four steps, not a smooth fade
                    picture.set_at((x, y), tuple(int(c * band * band) for c in color))
        _glows[key] = picture
    return _glows[key]


def glow(surface, center, radius, color, strength=1.0):
    """Add a spot of light to the surface, centred on `center`. `strength` from 0 to 1 dims it."""
    strength = max(0.0, min(1.0, strength))
    if strength <= 0 or radius < 1:
        return
    dimmed = tuple(int(c * strength) for c in color)
    picture = glow_picture(radius, dimmed)
    surface.blit(picture, (center[0] - radius, center[1] - radius), special_flags=pygame.BLEND_RGB_ADD)


def pulse(frame, speed, low=0.0, high=1.0, phase=0.0):
    """A value going smoothly between low and high and back, `speed` radians per frame."""
    return low + (high - low) * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(frame * speed + phase))


# --- Dissolving pictures pixel by pixel ----------------------------------------------------

_thresholds = {}


def threshold(x, y):
    """A fixed number between 0 and 1 for each pixel, scattered: the order pixels dissolve in."""
    key = (x, y)
    if key not in _thresholds:
        value = (x * 73856093) ^ (y * 19349663) ^ 0x5bd1e995
        _thresholds[key] = (value % 9973) / 9973
    return _thresholds[key]


def dissolved(picture, progress, appearing=False):
    """A copy of a picture with part of its pixels gone: none at progress 0, all at 1.

    With `appearing`, the other way round: the picture builds up, pixel by pixel.
    """
    result = pygame.Surface(picture.get_size(), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    result.blit(picture, (0, 0))
    width, height = result.get_size()
    for y in range(height):
        for x in range(width):
            gone = threshold(x, y) < progress
            if appearing:
                gone = not gone
            if gone:
                result.set_at((x, y), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    return result


# --- The floor swaying around a portal ------------------------------------------------------


def ripple(surface, area, frame, amplitude=PORTAL_RIPPLE):
    """Shift the rows of an area of the surface sideways in a slow wave: the floor sways.

    Neighbouring rows move almost together, so lines on the floor bend instead of
    breaking; the top and bottom rows of the area stay still, so its edges never show.
    """
    area = area.clip(surface.get_rect())
    if area.width <= 0 or area.height <= 0 or amplitude <= 0:
        return
    copy = surface.subsurface(area).copy()
    for row in range(area.height):
        calm = math.sin(math.pi * row / max(area.height - 1, 1))  # 0 at the edges, 1 in the middle
        shift = round(amplitude * calm * math.sin(frame * 0.08 + row * 0.22))
        if shift:
            surface.blit(copy, (area.left + shift, area.top + row), pygame.Rect(0, row, area.width, 1))


def draw_ring_light(surface, center, radius, frame, speed=PORTAL_SPIN):
    """Three bright arcs turning slowly around a ring, and sparkles turning the other way inside it."""
    for arc in range(3):
        start = frame * speed + arc * 2 * math.pi / 3
        for step in range(7):
            angle = start - step * 0.09
            x = center[0] + round(radius * math.cos(angle))
            y = center[1] + round(radius * math.sin(angle))
            color = PURPLE[1] if step < 2 else PURPLE[0]
            surface.fill(color, (x, y, 1, 1))
    for spark in range(5):
        angle = -frame * speed * 1.6 + spark * 2 * math.pi / 5
        x = center[0] + round(radius * 0.45 * math.cos(angle))
        y = center[1] + round(radius * 0.45 * math.sin(angle))
        surface.fill(ENERGY[1], (x, y, 1, 1))


def draw_warp(screen, view, fx):
    """The purple warp after a portal: the view waves, a purple veil fades, particles fly out."""
    if fx["warp"] <= 0:
        return
    left = fx["warp"] / WARP_FRAMES  # from 1, just arrived, down to 0
    amplitude = round(6 * left)
    if amplitude:
        copy = screen.subsurface(view).copy()
        for row in range(view.height):
            shift = round(amplitude * math.sin(row * 0.18 + fx["warp"] * 0.5))
            screen.blit(copy, (view.left + shift, view.top + row), pygame.Rect(0, row, view.width, 1))
    veil = pygame.Surface(view.size, pygame.SRCALPHA)
    veil.fill((112, 40, 176, int(200 * left)))
    screen.blit(veil, view.topleft)
    if fx["warp"] % 2 == 0:
        spawn(fx, view.centerx, view.centery, 6, PURPLE, speed=3.0, life=26, layer="screen")
    draw_particles(screen, fx["screen"])


def draw_clue_glow(surface, fx):
    """A new clue: a soft glow on the thing that gave it, swelling then fading, and four short rays
    turning around it like a twinkling star."""
    clue = fx["clue"]
    if clue is None:
        return
    progress = clue["age"] / CLUE_FRAMES
    strength = math.sin(math.pi * min(1.0, progress * 1.4))  # swells quickly, fades slowly
    center = clue["rect"].center
    glow(surface, center, CLUE_GLOW, (110, 95, 40), strength)
    length = round(3 + 6 * strength)
    turn = clue["age"] * 0.06
    for ray in range(4):
        angle = turn + ray * math.pi / 2
        for step in range(2, length):
            x = center[0] + round(step * math.cos(angle))
            y = center[1] + round(step * math.sin(angle))
            surface.fill(CLUE[1] if step < length - 1 else CLUE[0], (x, y, 1, 1))


def draw_flash(screen, view, fx, color=(255, 255, 255)):
    """A short white flash over the view, fading out."""
    if fx["flash"] <= 0:
        return
    veil = pygame.Surface(view.size, pygame.SRCALPHA)
    veil.fill(color + (int(220 * fx["flash"] / FLASH_FRAMES),))
    screen.blit(veil, view.topleft)


================================================================================
# FILE: plot_twist/sprites.py
================================================================================

"""The pixel art of Plot Twist: every sprite and tile, drawn as text.

Each picture is a list of rows, and each character of a row is one pixel: the
PALETTE says its color, and "." is transparent. To change a picture, change its
letters; every row of a picture must be as long as the others.

Names follow what main.py looks for:
- an item, puzzle or character by its id, or by id and status ("hold_puzzle_solved"),
  or else by kind and status ("switch_on"); the barriers as "barrier_0" and "barrier_1";
- the numbered tiles as "tile_lit", "tile_dark" and "tile_error" (main.py writes the
  number on them), on the metal "zone" of their floor puzzle;
- doors as "door_open", "door_barred", "door_locked" and "door_sealed", drawn for
  the north wall (main.py turns them for the other walls);
- the player as "player_<down/up/right>_<0, 1 or 2>": 0 standing, 1 and 2 walking
  (left is right mirrored);
- the room tiles as "floor_<room>" and "wall_<room>", with "_0" to "_3" for the
  pictures of a moving tile (the stormy sea around the deck); "sea_calm_0" and
  "sea_calm_1" are the calm sea of the good ending.
- decor as "decor_<picture>", shared by every piece that names it in story.py
  (as big as the piece: the crab statue is 2 x 2 tiles), and the "portal";
A picture missing here is drawn as a colored rectangle by main.py.
"""

import pygame

PALETTE = {
    "k": (20, 16, 28),     # outline, black
    "w": (244, 240, 228),  # white
    "l": (176, 176, 190),  # light gray
    "d": (96, 96, 112),    # dark gray
    "r": (214, 64, 52),    # red
    "R": (130, 30, 34),    # dark red
    "o": (240, 140, 50),   # orange
    "y": (252, 214, 80),   # yellow
    "Y": (190, 140, 40),   # brass
    "b": (186, 128, 70),   # light wood
    "B": (140, 90, 50),    # wood
    "n": (88, 56, 34),     # dark wood
    "s": (40, 88, 168),    # sea
    "S": (24, 52, 112),    # deep sea
    "f": (150, 196, 236),  # foam
    "c": (112, 232, 255),  # magic
    "C": (56, 148, 200),   # dark magic
    "p": (186, 116, 255),  # purple
    "P": (112, 62, 176),   # dark purple
    "z": (250, 200, 160),  # skin
    "Z": (214, 150, 110),  # skin shadow
    "h": (96, 62, 40),     # hair
    "g": (96, 200, 120),   # green
    "G": (52, 128, 80),    # dark green
    "j": (60, 80, 150),    # trousers
    "t": (70, 128, 128),   # teal

# The second character: the same pictures as the player, with a red shirt instead of a green one.
for _name in [name for name in SPRITES if name.startswith("player_")]:
    SPRITES["player_red_" + _name[len("player_"):]] = [row.translate(str.maketrans("gG", "rR")) for row in SPRITES[_name]]

# Some rooms reuse the wood of the cabin and the planks of the hold.
SPRITES["floor_galley"] = SPRITES["floor_cabin"]
SPRITES["wall_galley"] = SPRITES["wall_hold"]
SPRITES["floor_helm"] = SPRITES["floor_cabin"]
SPRITES["wall_helm"] = SPRITES["wall_cabin"]
SPRITES["floor_east_room"] = SPRITES["floor_cabin"]
SPRITES["wall_east_room"] = SPRITES["wall_cabin"]
SPRITES["floor_corridor"] = SPRITES["floor_hold"]
SPRITES["wall_corridor"] = SPRITES["wall_hold"]
SPRITES["floor_corridor_end"] = SPRITES["floor_hold"]
SPRITES["wall_corridor_end"] = SPRITES["wall_hold"]

# Pictures already turned into pygame Surfaces: (name, turns, flip) -> Surface.
_images = {}


def image(name, turns=0, flip=False):
    """The picture called `name` as a Surface, or None if there is no such picture.

    `turns` turns it a quarter turn counterclockwise each, `flip` mirrors it left
    to right. Each picture is drawn pixel by pixel only the first time.
    """
    if name not in SPRITES:
        return None
    key = (name, turns % 4, flip)
    if key not in _images:
        picture = build(SPRITES[name])
        if flip:
            picture = pygame.transform.flip(picture, True, False)
        if turns % 4:
            picture = pygame.transform.rotate(picture, 90 * (turns % 4))
        _images[key] = picture
    return _images[key]


def build(rows):
    """Draw a picture from its rows of letters, on a transparent Surface."""
    picture = pygame.Surface((len(rows[0]), len(rows)), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for y, row in enumerate(rows):
        for x, letter in enumerate(row):
            if letter != ".":
                picture.set_at((x, y), PALETTE[letter])
    return picture

# FILE: plot_twist/pixel_font.py

"""The pixel font of Plot Twist, drawn as text like the sprites.

Every character is a small picture 9 pixels tall, where "#" is a pixel and "."
is empty. Capitals and digits use the top 7 rows, small letters rows 2 to 6,
and g, j, p, q, y go down into the last two rows. Accents sit above the letters.

The characters are written in "sheets": the characters in a string, and below
them their pictures side by side, separated by spaces. A sheet of 7 rows gets
two empty rows added at the bottom.

PixelFont can be used like pygame.font.Font: render(), size() and get_linesize().
"""

import pygame

HEIGHT = 9      # rows of every character
SPACING = 1     # empty columns between two characters
LINE_GAP = 2    # empty rows between two lines of text

SHEETS = [

# What is drawn for a character the font does not have: a small empty box.
MISSING = ["####", "#..#", "#..#", "#..#", "#..#", "#..#", "####", "....", "...."]


def read_sheets(sheets):
    """Turn the sheets into a dict: character -> list of HEIGHT rows of "#" and "."."""
    glyphs = {}
    for chars, block in sheets:
        rows = [row.split() for row in block.strip("\n").split("\n")]
        while len(rows) < HEIGHT:
            rows.append(["." * len(picture) for picture in rows[0]])
        for row in rows:
            if len(row) != len(chars):
                raise ValueError(f"the sheet {chars!r} has a row with {len(row)} characters instead of {len(chars)}")
        for index, char in enumerate(chars):
            glyphs[char] = [row[index] for row in rows]
    return glyphs


GLYPHS = read_sheets(SHEETS)


def glyph(char):
    """The picture of a character, or the empty box if the font does not have it."""
    return GLYPHS.get(char, MISSING)


class PixelFont:
    """The pixel font at a scale (1, 2, 3...), used like a pygame.font.Font.

    A class keeps together the scale and the texts already drawn, so that each
    text is drawn pixel by pixel only once.
    """

    def __init__(self, scale=1):
        self.scale = scale
        self.drawn = {}

    def get_linesize(self):
        """The distance between two lines of text, in pixels."""
        return (HEIGHT + LINE_GAP) * self.scale

    def size(self, text):
        """The (width, height) in pixels of a line of text."""
        width = 0
        for char in text:
            width += len(glyph(char)[0]) + SPACING
        if text:
            width -= SPACING
        return (width * self.scale, HEIGHT * self.scale)

    def render(self, text, antialias, color):
        """A transparent picture of the text in `color`. `antialias` is ignored: the pixels stay sharp."""
        key = (text, tuple(color))
        if key not in self.drawn:
            if len(self.drawn) > 500:
                self.drawn.clear()  # many different texts: start over rather than keep them all
            self.drawn[key] = self.draw(text, color)
        return self.drawn[key]

    def draw(self, text, color):
        width, height = self.size(text)
        picture = pygame.Surface((max(width, 1), height), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        scale = self.scale
        x = 0
        for char in text:
            rows = glyph(char)
            for y, row in enumerate(rows):
                for column, pixel in enumerate(row):
                    if pixel == "#":
                        picture.fill(color, (x + column * scale, y * scale, scale, scale))
            x += (len(rows[0]) + SPACING) * scale
        return picture


================================================================================
# FILE: plot_twist/sounds.py
================================================================================

"""The sound effects of Plot Twist, made from square waves like on an 8-bit console.

There are no sound files: each effect is a list of notes, (frequency in Hz,
length in seconds), where a frequency of 0 is a pause. load() turns them into
pygame sounds once the mixer is running, in whatever rate and channels it opened
with. Without audio (no sound card, or the mixer could not start), play() does
nothing, and the game runs the same.
"""

import array

import pygame

VOLUME = 0.2  # of the loudest square wave: the effects are short and should not hurt
FADE = 0.005  # seconds at the end of each note that fade out, so notes do not click

EFFECTS = {
    "blip": [(1047, 0.04)],                                            # a tile lights up
    "ding": [(784, 0.07), (1175, 0.16)],                               # a phase is complete
    "fanfare": [(523, 0.08), (659, 0.08), (784, 0.08), (1047, 0.3)],   # the puzzle is solved
    "buzz": [(98, 0.12), (0, 0.04), (98, 0.3)],                        # BZZZ: a mistake, the lights go out
    "clue": [(988, 0.05), (1319, 0.05), (0, 0.02), (1976, 0.22)],      # a new clue: a light, rising chime
}

_loaded = {}  # effect name -> pygame Sound, once load() has run with audio


def samples(notes, rate, channels=1):
    """The notes as signed 16-bit samples, the same value repeated on every channel."""
    peak = int(32767 * VOLUME)
    fade = max(1, int(rate * FADE))
    data = array.array("h")
    for frequency, length in notes:
        count = int(rate * length)
        for index in range(count):
            value = 0
            if frequency > 0:
                # Half of each period up, half down: a square wave.
                up = (2 * index * frequency // rate) % 2 == 0
                value = peak if up else -peak
                left = count - index
                if left < fade:
                    value = value * left // fade
            data.extend([value] * channels)
    return data


def load():
    """Make the sounds for the running mixer. Without audio, or with samples that are not 16-bit, there are none."""
    _loaded.clear()
    settings = pygame.mixer.get_init()
    if settings is None:
        return
    rate, size, channels = settings
    if size != -16:
        return
    for name, notes in EFFECTS.items():
        _loaded[name] = pygame.mixer.Sound(buffer=samples(notes, rate, channels).tobytes())


def play(name):
    """Play an effect. An unknown name, None or a game without audio make no sound."""
    sound = _loaded.get(name)
    if sound is not None:
        sound.play()

# FILE: plot_twist/conftest.py
================================================================================

"""Shared test setup: no test ever touches the real save file."""

import pytest

import save


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def save_file(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Point save.SAVE_PATH to a fresh temporary folder for every test, and return that file's path.

    The file does not exist until a test saves something.
    """
    path = tmp_path / "savegame.json"
    monkeypatch.setattr(save, "SAVE_PATH", str(path))
    return path

# FILE: plot_twist/test_engine.py
